<
A class to describe an identifiable collection of one or more goods items to be transported
               between the consignor and the consignee. This information may be defined within a
               transport contract. A consignment may comprise more than one shipment (e.g., when
               consolidated by a freight forwarder).
            
Consignment. Details
2.0
ABIE
Consignment
345

GrossTonnageMeasure
Gross tonnage is calculated by measuring a ship's volume (from keel to funnel, to
               the outside of the hull framing) and applying a mathematical formula and is used to
               determine things such as a ship's manning regulations, safety rules, registration
               fees and port dues.
